use mdns_sd::{ServiceDaemon, ServiceInfo};

pub fn publier(port: u16) -> Option<ServiceDaemon> {
    let daemon = ServiceDaemon::new().ok()?;
    let hostname = "omniprint.local.";
    let instance = "OmniPrint";
    let service_type = "_http._tcp.local.";
    let properties = [("path", "/"), ("version", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"))];
    let ip = crate::reseau_utils::adresse_ip_locale();
    let info = ServiceInfo::new(service_type, instance, hostname, ip.as_str(), port, &properties[..]).ok()?;
    daemon.register(info).ok()?;
    Some(daemon)
}

--- FICHIER : D:\Creation\Rust\OmniPrint\src\pare_feu.rs ---
use std::os::windows::process::CommandExt;
use std::process::Command;

const CREATE_NO_WINDOW: u32 = 0x0800_0000;

pub fn assurer_regle(port: u16) -> Result<(), String> {
    let exe = std::env::current_exe()
        .map_err(|e| format!("Chemin de l'exécutable introuvable : {}", e))?;
    let exe_str = exe.to_string_lossy().to_string();

    if regle_existe(port) {
        return Ok(());
    }

    let _ = supprimer_regles();

    if ajouter_regle_directe(&exe_str, port).is_ok() {
        if regle_existe(port) {
            return Ok(());
        }
    }

    ajouter_regle_uac(&exe_str, port)
}

fn regle_existe(port: u16) -> bool {
    let sortie = Command::new("netsh")
        .args([
            "advfirewall",
            "firewall",
            "show",
            "rule",
            "name=OmniPrint",
        ])
        .creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
        .output();
    let Ok(o) = sortie else { return false; };
    if !o.status.success() {
        return false;
    }
    let texte = String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).to_lowercase();
    let port_str = format!("localport:{}", port);
    texte.contains(&port_str) || texte.contains(&format!("{}", port))
}

fn supprimer_regles() -> Result<(), String> {
    let sortie = Command::new("netsh")
        .args([
            "advfirewall",
            "firewall",
            "delete",
            "rule",
            "name=OmniPrint",
        ])
        .creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
        .output();
    match sortie {
        Ok(_) => Ok(()),
        Err(e) => Err(format!("netsh indisponible : {}", e)),
    }
}

fn ajouter_regle_directe(exe: &str, port: u16) -> Result<(), String> {
    let prog_arg = format!("program=\"{}\"", exe);
    let port_arg = format!("localport={}", port);
    let sortie = Command::new("netsh")
        .args([
            "advfirewall",
            "firewall",
            "add",
            "rule",
            "name=OmniPrint",
            "dir=in",
            "action=allow",
            &prog_arg,
            "protocol=TCP",
            &port_arg,
            "enable=yes",
            "profile=private,domain,public",
        ])
        .creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
        .output();
    match sortie {
        Ok(o) if o.status.success() => Ok(()),
        Ok(_) => Err("Droits administrateur requis".into()),
        Err(e) => Err(format!("netsh indisponible : {}", e)),
    }
}

fn ajouter_regle_uac(exe: &str, port: u16) -> Result<(), String> {
    let prog_arg = format!("program=\"{}\"", exe.replace('"', ""));
    let port_arg = format!("localport={}", port);
    let script = format!(
        "try {{ Start-Process -FilePath 'netsh' -ArgumentList 'advfirewall','firewall','add','rule','name=OmniPrint','dir=in','action=allow','{}','protocol=TCP','{}','enable=yes','profile=private,domain,public' -Verb RunAs -Wait -WindowStyle Hidden; exit 0 }} catch {{ Write-Output $_.Exception.Message; exit 1 }}",
        prog_arg.replace('\'', "''"),
        port_arg
    );
    let sortie = Command::new("powershell")
        .args([
            "-NoProfile",
            "-NonInteractive",
            "-WindowStyle",
            "Hidden",
            "-Command",
            &script,
        ])
        .creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
        .output();
    match sortie {
        Ok(o) if o.status.success() => {
            if regle_existe(port) {
                Ok(())
            } else {
                Err("Règle non créée après élévation".into())
            }
        }
        Ok(o) => {
            let msg = String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string();
            if msg.is_empty() {
                Err("Demande d'élévation refusée".into())
            } else {
                Err(msg)
            }
        }
        Err(e) => Err(format!("PowerShell indisponible : {}", e)),
    }
}

pub fn supprimer_toutes_regles() {
    let _ = Command::new("netsh")
        .args([
            "advfirewall",
            "firewall",
            "delete",
            "rule",
            "name=OmniPrint",
        ])
        .creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
        .output();
}

--- FICHIER : D:\Creation\Rust\OmniPrint\src\partage.rs ---
use std::os::windows::process::CommandExt;
use std::process::Command;

const CREATE_NO_WINDOW: u32 = 0x0800_0000;

pub fn nom_pc() -> String {
    std::env::var("COMPUTERNAME").unwrap_or_else(|_| "PC".into())
}

pub fn chemin_unc(nom_partage: &str) -> String {
    format!("\\\\{}\\{}", nom_pc(), nom_partage)
}

fn powershell(script: &str) -> std::io::Result<std::process::Output> {
    Command::new("powershell")
        .args([
            "-NoProfile",
            "-ExecutionPolicy",
            "Bypass",
            "-WindowStyle",
            "Hidden",
            "-Command",
            script,
        ])
        .creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
        .output()
}

fn echapper(v: &str) -> String {
    v.replace('\'', "''")
}

fn est_eleve() -> bool {
    let script = "([Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal][Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent()).IsInRole([Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)";
    match powershell(script) {
        Ok(o) if o.status.success() => {
            String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case("True")
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}

pub fn partage_actif(nom_imprimante: &str) -> bool {
    if nom_imprimante.is_empty() {
        return false;
    }
    let nom = echapper(nom_imprimante);
    let script = format!(
        "$p = Get-Printer -Name '{}' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue; if ($p -ne $null -and $p.Shared -eq $true) {{ 'OUI' }} else {{ 'NON' }}",
        nom
    );
    if let Ok(o) = powershell(&script) {
        if o.status.success() {
            let t = String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout);
            return t.contains("OUI");
        }
    }
    false
}

fn script_activer(imprimante: &str, nom_partage: &str) -> String {
    let imp = echapper(imprimante);
    let part = echapper(nom_partage);
    format!(
        r#"$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
try {{
    $profil = (Get-NetConnectionProfile -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 1)
    if ($profil -ne $null -and $profil.NetworkCategory -ne 'Private') {{
        Set-NetConnectionProfile -InterfaceIndex $profil.InterfaceIndex -NetworkCategory Private -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    }}
    Set-NetFirewallRule -DisplayGroup 'Découverte du réseau' -Enabled True -Profile Domain,Private -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Set-NetFirewallRule -DisplayGroup 'Partage de fichiers et d’imprimantes' -Enabled True -Profile Domain,Private -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Set-NetFirewallRule -DisplayGroup 'Network Discovery' -Enabled True -Profile Domain,Private -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Set-NetFirewallRule -DisplayGroup 'File and Printer Sharing' -Enabled True -Profile Domain,Private -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Set-Service -Name Spooler -StartupType Automatic -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Start-Service -Name Spooler -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    $p = Get-Printer -Name '{imp}' -ErrorAction Stop
    if ($p.Shared) {{ Set-Printer -Name '{imp}' -Shared $false }}
    Set-Printer -Name '{imp}' -Shared $true -ShareName '{part}' -Published $true
    try {{
        $drivers = Get-PrinterDriver -Name $p.DriverName -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        if ($drivers -ne $null) {{
            Add-PrinterDriver -Name $p.DriverName -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        }}
    }} catch {{}}
    'OK'
}} catch {{
    Write-Output ('ERREUR: ' + $_.Exception.Message)
    exit 1
}}"#
    )
}

pub fn activer_partage(imprimante: &str, nom_partage: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    if imprimante.is_empty() {
        return Err("Aucune imprimante sélectionnée.".into());
    }
    if nom_partage.is_empty() {
        return Err("Nom de partage vide.".into());
    }
    if partage_actif(imprimante) {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let script = script_activer(imprimante, nom_partage);
    let sortie = powershell(&script);
    match sortie {
        Ok(o) if o.status.success() => {
            if partage_actif(imprimante) {
                Ok(())
            } else {
                Err("Partage non confirmé après exécution.".into())
            }
        }
        Ok(o) => {
            let msg = String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string();
            if est_eleve() {
                if msg.is_empty() {
                    Err("Le script de partage a échoué sans message. Vérifiez que le service Spouleur est démarré.".into())
                } else {
                    Err(msg)
                }
            } else if msg.contains("Access is denied") || msg.contains("accès") || msg.contains("élevation") || msg.is_empty() {
                activer_partage_uac(imprimante, nom_partage)
            } else {
                Err(msg)
            }
        }
        Err(e) => Err(format!("PowerShell indisponible : {}", e)),
    }
}

fn activer_partage_uac(imprimante: &str, nom_partage: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    let script = script_activer(imprimante, nom_partage);
    let script_echappe = script.replace('\'', "''");
    let commande = format!(
        "try {{ Start-Process -FilePath 'powershell' -ArgumentList '-NoProfile','-ExecutionPolicy','Bypass','-Command','{}' -Verb RunAs -Wait; exit 0 }} catch {{ Write-Output $_.Exception.Message; exit 1 }}",
        script_echappe
    );
    let sortie = Command::new("powershell")
        .args([
            "-NoProfile",
            "-ExecutionPolicy",
            "Bypass",
            "-Command",
            &commande,
        ])
        .output();
    match sortie {
        Ok(o) if o.status.success() => {
            if partage_actif(imprimante) {
                Ok(())
            } else {
                Err("Élévation accordée mais partage non confirmé. Vérifiez que le service Spouleur est démarré.".into())
            }
        }
        Ok(o) => {
            let msg = String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string();
            if msg.is_empty() {
                Err("Demande d'élévation refusée.".into())
            } else {
                Err(msg)
            }
        }
        Err(e) => Err(format!("PowerShell indisponible : {}", e)),
    }
}

--- FICHIER : D:\Creation\Rust\OmniPrint\src\plage_pages.rs ---
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ModePages {
    Toutes,
    Personnalise,
}

impl Default for ModePages {
    fn default() -> Self {
        ModePages::Toutes
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SelectionPages {
    pub mode: ModePages,
    pub pages_cochees: Vec<u32>,
    pub copies: u32,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub est_feuille: bool,
}

impl Default for SelectionPages {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            mode: ModePages::Toutes,
            pages_cochees: Vec::new(),
            copies: 1,
            est_feuille: false,
        }
    }
}

impl SelectionPages {
    pub fn resoudre(&self, total: u32) -> Result<Vec<u32>, String> {
        match self.mode {
            ModePages::Toutes => Ok((1..=total).collect()),
            ModePages::Personnalise => {
                if self.pages_cochees.is_empty() {
                    return Err(if self.est_feuille {
                        "Aucune feuille sélectionnée.".into()
                    } else {
                        "Aucune page sélectionnée.".into()
                    });
                }
                let mut v: Vec<u32> = self
                    .pages_cochees
                    .iter()
                    .copied()
                    .filter(|p| *p >= 1 && *p <= total)
                    .collect();
                v.sort_unstable();
                v.dedup();
                if v.is_empty() {
                    return Err(if self.est_feuille {
                        "Aucune feuille valide.".into()
                    } else {
                        "Aucune page valide.".into()
                    });
                }
                Ok(v)
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn spec_imprimante(&self, total: u32) -> Result<String, String> {
        let pages = self.resoudre(total)?;
        if self.mode == ModePages::Toutes {
            return Ok(String::new());
        }
        Ok(formater_pages(&pages))
    }

    pub fn copies_effectives(&self) -> u32 {
        if self.copies == 0 { 1 } else { self.copies }
    }

    pub fn pages_effectives(&self, total: u32) -> u32 {
        match self.mode {
            ModePages::Toutes => total,
            ModePages::Personnalise => {
                self.pages_cochees
                    .iter()
                    .filter(|p| **p >= 1 && **p <= total)
                    .count() as u32
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn resume(&self, total: u32) -> String {
        let mot = if self.est_feuille { "feuille(s)" } else { "page(s)" };
        match self.mode {
            ModePages::Toutes => {
                if total > 0 {
                    format!("Toutes ({} {})", total, mot)
                } else {
                    format!("Toutes les {}", mot)
                }
            },
            ModePages::Personnalise => {
                if self.pages_cochees.is_empty() {
                    "Sélection vide".into()
                } else {
                    let mut v = self.pages_cochees.clone();
                    v.sort_unstable();
                    v.dedup();
                    format!("Sélection : {}", formater_pages(&v))
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

pub fn formater_pages(pages: &[u32]) -> String {
    if pages.is_empty() {
        return String::new();
    }
    let mut triees = pages.to_vec();
    triees.sort_unstable();
    triees.dedup();
    let mut morceaux: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < triees.len() {
        let debut = triees[i];
        let mut fin = debut;
        while i + 1 < triees.len() && triees[i + 1] == fin + 1 {
            fin = triees[i + 1];
            i += 1;
        }
        if debut == fin {
            morceaux.push(format!("{}", debut));
        } else {
            morceaux.push(format!("{}-{}", debut, fin));
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    morceaux.join(",")
}

--- FICHIER : D:\Creation\Rust\OmniPrint\src\printer_driver_check.rs ---
use std::os::windows::process::CommandExt;
use std::process::Command;

const CREATE_NO_WINDOW: u32 = 0x08000000;

pub fn driver_exists(driver_name: &str) -> bool {
    let script = format!(
        "if (Get-PrinterDriver -Name '{}' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue) {{ 'YES' }} else {{ 'NO' }}",
        driver_name
    );
    Command::new("powershell")
        .args(["-NoProfile", "-Command", &script])
        .creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
        .output()
        .map(|o| String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).contains("YES"))
        .unwrap_or(false)
}

pub fn ensure_driver_installed(driver_name: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    if driver_exists(driver_name) {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(format!(
            "Pilote '{}' absent, imprimante virtuelle non créée.",
            driver_name
        ))
    }
}

--- FICHIER : D:\Creation\Rust\OmniPrint\src\printer.rs ---
use std::io::Read;
use std::os::windows::process::CommandExt;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::Command;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use crate::journal::Statut;
use crate::session::{ModeCouleur, Orientation};

const CREATE_NO_WINDOW: u32 = 0x0800_0000;
const TIMEOUT_COM: u64 = 90;

fn powershell_invisible(script: &str) -> std::io::Result<std::process::Output> {
    Command::new("powershell")
        .args(["-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-WindowStyle", "Hidden", "-Command", script])
        .creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
        .output()
}

pub fn lister_imprimantes() -> Vec<String> {
    let mut resultat = Vec::new();
    let script = "Get-Printer | Select-Object -ExpandProperty Name";
    if let Ok(out) = powershell_invisible(script) {
        if out.status.success() {
            let texte = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
            for ligne in texte.lines() {
                let l = ligne.trim().trim_start_matches('\u{feff}');
                if !l.is_empty() {
                    resultat.push(l.to_string());
                }
            }
        }
    }
    resultat
}

fn tuer_processus(noms: &[&str]) {
    for nom in noms {
        let _ = Command::new("taskkill")
            .args(["/F", "/IM", nom])
            .creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
            .output();
    }
}

pub fn annuler_impression() {
    tuer_processus(&["SumatraPDF.exe"]);
}

pub fn trouver_sumatra() -> Option<PathBuf> {
    let exe_dir = std::env::current_exe().ok()?.parent()?.to_path_buf();
    let candidats: Vec<PathBuf> = vec![
        exe_dir.join("bin").join("SumatraPDF.exe"),
        exe_dir.join("SumatraPDF.exe"),
        std::env::var("LOCALAPPDATA")
            .map(|p| PathBuf::from(p).join("SumatraPDF").join("SumatraPDF.exe"))
            .unwrap_or_default(),
        PathBuf::from(r"C:\Program Files\SumatraPDF\SumatraPDF.exe"),
        PathBuf::from(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\SumatraPDF\SumatraPDF.exe"),
    ];
    for c in candidats {
        if !c.as_os_str().is_empty() && c.exists() {
            return Some(c);
        }
    }
    if let Ok(p) = std::env::var("PATH") {
        for dir in p.split(';') {
            let cand = Path::new(dir).join("SumatraPDF.exe");
            if cand.exists() {
                return Some(cand);
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn est_document_word(extension: &str) -> bool {
    matches!(
        extension,
        "doc" | "docx" | "docm" | "dot" | "dotx" | "dotm" | "rtf" | "odt"
    )
}

fn est_document_excel(extension: &str) -> bool {
    matches!(
        extension,
        "xlsx" | "xlsm" | "xltx" | "xltm" | "xls" | "xlsb" | "ods" | "csv"
    )
}

fn echapper_chaine_powershell(valeur: &str) -> String {
    valeur.replace('\'', "''")
}

fn convertir_office_en_pdf(chemin: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    let destination = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
        "omniprint_office_{}.pdf",
        uuid::Uuid::new_v4()
    ));
    let resultat = office2pdf::convert(chemin)
        .map_err(|e| format!("Conversion Office en PDF impossible : {:?}", e))?;
    std::fs::write(&destination, &resultat.pdf)
        .map_err(|e| format!("Ecriture PDF temporaire impossible : {}", e))?;
    Ok(destination)
}

fn rendre_page_pdf_pure(pdf: &Path, page: u32, sortie: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
    use hayro::hayro_interpret::InterpreterSettings;
    use hayro::hayro_syntax::Pdf;
    use hayro::{render, RenderSettings};

    let donnees = std::fs::read(pdf).map_err(|e| format!("Lecture PDF impossible : {}", e))?;
    let data: std::sync::Arc<dyn AsRef<[u8]> + Send + Sync> = std::sync::Arc::new(donnees);
    let pdf_doc = Pdf::new(data).map_err(|e| format!("Parsing PDF impossible : {:?}", e))?;
    let pages = pdf_doc.pages();
    let index = (page as usize).saturating_sub(1);
    let page_ref = pages
        .get(index)
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("Page {} introuvable", page))?;

    let settings = RenderSettings {
        x_scale: 96.0 / 72.0,
        y_scale: 96.0 / 72.0,
        ..Default::default()
    };

    let pixmap = render(page_ref, &InterpreterSettings::default(), &settings);

    let largeur = pixmap.width() as u32;
    let hauteur = pixmap.height() as u32;
    let data_rgba = pixmap.data_as_u8_slice();
    let image = image::RgbaImage::from_raw(largeur, hauteur, data_rgba.to_vec())
        .ok_or_else(|| "Conversion Pixmap -> RgbaImage impossible".to_string())?;
    image
        .save(sortie)
        .map_err(|e| format!("Ecriture PNG impossible : {}", e))?;
    Ok(())
}

pub fn generer_apercu(chemin_fichier: &PathBuf, page: u32) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    let extension = chemin_fichier
        .extension()
        .map(|e| e.to_string_lossy().to_lowercase())
        .unwrap_or_default();

    let (chemin_pdf, est_temporaire) = if extension == "pdf" {
        (chemin_fichier.clone(), false)
    } else if est_document_word(&extension) || est_document_excel(&extension) {
        (convertir_office_en_pdf(chemin_fichier)?, true)
    } else {
        return Err("Format non supporté pour l'aperçu.".into());
    };

    let fichier_sortie = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("omniprint_apercu_{}.png", uuid::Uuid::new_v4()));
    let resultat = rendre_page_pdf_pure(&chemin_pdf, page, &fichier_sortie);

    if est_temporaire {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&chemin_pdf);
    }

    resultat.map(|_| fichier_sortie)
}

pub fn imprimer_fichier(
    nom_imprimante: &str,
    chemin_fichier: &PathBuf,
    journal_id: String,
    journal: Arc<Mutex<crate::journal::Journal>>,
    mode_couleur: ModeCouleur,
    orientation: Orientation,
    pages_spec: String,
    copies: u32,
    _est_feuille: bool,
) {
    let nom_imprimante = nom_imprimante.to_string();
    let chemin = chemin_fichier.clone();
    let monochrome = matches!(mode_couleur, ModeCouleur::NoirBlanc);
    let copies = if copies == 0 { 1 } else { copies };

    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        {
            let mut j = journal.lock().unwrap();
            j.mettre_a_jour_statut(&journal_id, Statut::EnCours, String::new());
        }

        let extension = chemin
            .extension()
            .map(|e| e.to_string_lossy().to_lowercase())
            .unwrap_or_default();

        let resultat = if est_document_excel(&extension) {
            let pdf = match convertir_office_en_pdf(&chemin) {
                Ok(p) => p,
                Err(e) => {
                    let mut j = journal.lock().unwrap();
                    j.mettre_a_jour_statut(&journal_id, Statut::Erreur, e);
                    return;
                }
            };
            let res = if let Some(sumatra) = trouver_sumatra() {
                let mut args = vec!["-print-to".to_string(), nom_imprimante.clone()];
                let mut reglages = Vec::new();
                if monochrome {
                    reglages.push("monochrome".to_string());
                }
                if copies > 1 {
                    reglages.push(format!("{}x", copies));
                }
                if !pages_spec.trim().is_empty() {
                    reglages.push(pages_spec.trim().to_string());
                }
                match orientation {
                    Orientation::Portrait => reglages.push("portrait".to_string()),
                    Orientation::Paysage => reglages.push("landscape".to_string()),
                }
                if !reglages.is_empty() {
                    args.push("-print-settings".into());
                    args.push(reglages.join(","));
                }
                args.push("-silent".into());
                args.push(pdf.to_string_lossy().to_string());
                match Command::new(&sumatra).args(&args).creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW).output() {
                    Ok(o) if o.status.success() => Ok("Document transmis a l'imprimante".to_string()),
                    Ok(o) => Err(format!("SumatraPDF a echoue (code {}).", o.status.code().unwrap_or(-1))),
                    Err(e) => Err(format!("Lancement de SumatraPDF impossible : {}", e)),
                }
            } else {
                Err("SumatraPDF est introuvable.".into())
            };
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&pdf);
            res
        } else {
            let (chemin_pdf, pdf_temporaire) = if extension == "pdf" {
                (Some(chemin.clone()), false)
            } else if est_document_word(&extension) {
                match convertir_office_en_pdf(&chemin) {
                    Ok(pdf) => (Some(pdf), true),
                    Err(e) => {
                        let mut j = journal.lock().unwrap();
                        j.mettre_a_jour_statut(&journal_id, Statut::Erreur, e);
                        return;
                    }
                }
            } else {
                (None, false)
            };

            let res = if let Some(pdf) = chemin_pdf.as_ref() {
                if let Some(sumatra) = trouver_sumatra() {
                    let mut args = vec!["-print-to".to_string(), nom_imprimante.clone()];
                    let mut reglages = Vec::new();
                    if monochrome {
                        reglages.push("monochrome".to_string());
                    }
                    if copies > 1 {
                        reglages.push(format!("{}x", copies));
                    }
                    if !pages_spec.trim().is_empty() {
                        reglages.push(pages_spec.trim().to_string());
                    }
                    match orientation {
                        Orientation::Portrait => reglages.push("portrait".to_string()),
                        Orientation::Paysage => reglages.push("landscape".to_string()),
                    }
                    if !reglages.is_empty() {
                        args.push("-print-settings".into());
                        args.push(reglages.join(","));
                    }
                    args.push("-silent".into());
                    args.push(pdf.to_string_lossy().to_string());
                    match Command::new(&sumatra).args(&args).creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW).output() {
                        Ok(o) if o.status.success() => Ok("Document transmis a l'imprimante".to_string()),
                        Ok(o) => Err(format!("SumatraPDF a echoue (code {}).", o.status.code().unwrap_or(-1))),
                        Err(e) => Err(format!("Lancement de SumatraPDF impossible : {}", e)),
                    }
                } else {
                    Err("SumatraPDF est introuvable.".into())
                }
            } else {
                let chemin_ps = echapper_chaine_powershell(&chemin.to_string_lossy());
                let script = if monochrome {
                    format!(
                        "$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'; try {{ Start-Process -FilePath '{0}' -Verb Print -ArgumentList 'monochrome'; exit 0 }} catch {{ Start-Process -FilePath '{0}' -Verb Print; exit 0 }}",
                        chemin_ps,
                    )
                } else {
                    format!(
                        "$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'; try {{ Start-Process -FilePath '{0}' -Verb Print; exit 0 }} catch {{ Write-Output $_.Exception.Message; exit 4 }}",
                        chemin_ps,
                    )
                };
                match powershell_invisible(&script) {
                    Ok(o) if o.status.success() => Ok("Document transmis a l'imprimante".to_string()),
                    Ok(o) => {
                        let msg = String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string();
                        Err(if msg.is_empty() { "Aucune application associee.".to_string() } else { msg })
                    }
                    Err(e) => Err(format!("Erreur systeme : {}", e)),
                }
            };

            if pdf_temporaire {
                if let Some(pdf) = chemin_pdf {
                    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(pdf);
                }
            }
            res
        };

        let (statut, message) = match resultat {
            Ok(message) => (Statut::Imprime, message),
            Err(message) => (Statut::Erreur, message),
        };
        let mut j = journal.lock().unwrap();
        j.mettre_a_jour_statut(&journal_id, statut, message);
    });
}

pub fn compter_pages_pdf(chemin: &PathBuf) -> u32 {
    if let Ok(document) = lopdf::Document::load(chemin) {
        let nombre = document.get_pages().len() as u32;
        if nombre > 0 {
            return nombre;
        }
    }
    let donnees = match std::fs::read(chemin) {
        Ok(donnees) => donnees,
        Err(_) => return 0,
    };
    let contenu = String::from_utf8_lossy(&donnees);
    let mut maximum = 0u32;
    let mut reste = contenu.as_ref();
    while let Some(position) = reste.find("/Count") {
        reste = &reste[position + "/Count".len()..];
        let valeur = reste.trim_start();
        let longueur_chiffres = valeur.chars().take_while(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()).map(char::len_utf8).sum::<usize>();
        if longueur_chiffres > 0 {
            if let Ok(nombre) = valeur[..longueur_chiffres].parse::<u32>() {
                maximum = maximum.max(nombre);
            }
        }
        let espaces = reste.len() - valeur.len();
        let avance = espaces + longueur_chiffres;
        if avance == 0 {
            let longueur = reste.chars().next().map(char::len_utf8).unwrap_or(0);
            if longueur == 0 { break; }
            reste = &reste[longueur..];
        } else {
            reste = &reste[avance..];
        }
    }
    maximum
}

fn lire_entree_zip(chemin: &Path, nom: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let fichier = std::fs::File::open(chemin).ok()?;
    let mut archive = zip::ZipArchive::new(fichier).ok()?;
    let mut entree = archive.by_name(nom).ok()?;
    let mut tampon = Vec::new();
    entree.read_to_end(&mut tampon).ok()?;
    Some(String::from_utf8_lossy(&tampon).to_string())
}

fn lire_slides_pptx(chemin: &Path) -> Option<u32> {
    let contenu = lire_entree_zip(chemin, "ppt/presentation.xml")?;
    let mut compte = 0u32;
    let mut reste = contenu.as_str();
    while let Some(pos) = reste.find("<p:sldId ") {
        compte += 1;
        reste = &reste[pos + 9..];
    }
    if compte > 0 { Some(compte) } else { None }
}

fn compter_pages_powerpoint(chemin: &str) -> Option<u32> {
    let chemin_esc = chemin.replace('\'', "''");
    let script = format!(
        r#"$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$p = $null
$pres = $null
try {{
  $p = New-Object -ComObject PowerPoint.Application
  $pres = $p.Presentations.Open('{chemin}', $true, $false, $false)
  $n = $pres.Slides.Count
  try {{ $pres.Close() }} catch {{}}
  try {{ $p.Quit() }} catch {{}}
  if ($n -gt 0) {{ Write-Output $n; exit 0 }}
  exit 1
}} catch {{
  if ($pres -ne $null) {{ try {{ $pres.Close() }} catch {{}} }}
  if ($p -ne $null) {{ try {{ $p.Quit() }} catch {{}} }}
  exit 1
}}"#,
        chemin = chemin_esc
    );
    let mut enfant = Command::new("powershell")
        .args(["-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-WindowStyle", "Hidden", "-Command", &script])
        .creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
        .stdout(std::process::Stdio::piped())
        .stderr(std::process::Stdio::piped())
        .spawn()
        .ok()?;
    let debut = Instant::now();
    loop {
        match enfant.try_wait() {
            Ok(Some(statut)) => {
                let sortie = enfant.wait_with_output().ok()?;
                if !statut.success() { return None; }
                let texte = String::from_utf8_lossy(&sortie.stdout).trim().to_string();
                return texte.parse::<u32>().ok();
            }
            Ok(None) => {
                if debut.elapsed() > Duration::from_secs(TIMEOUT_COM) {
                    let _ = enfant.kill();
                    tuer_processus(&["POWERPNT.EXE"]);
                    return None;
                }
                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(150));
            }
            Err(_) => return None,
        }
    }
}

pub fn compter_pages_document(chemin: &PathBuf, _imprimante: &str) -> Result<u32, String> {
    let extension = chemin.extension().map(|e| e.to_string_lossy().to_lowercase()).unwrap_or_default();
    let chemin_str = chemin.to_string_lossy().to_string();

    let nombre = if extension == "pdf" {
        compter_pages_pdf(chemin)
    } else if est_document_word(&extension) || est_document_excel(&extension) {
        let pdf = convertir_office_en_pdf(chemin)?;
        let nombre = compter_pages_pdf(&pdf);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&pdf);
        nombre
    } else {
        match extension.as_str() {
            "pptx" | "pptm" | "potx" | "potm" => lire_slides_pptx(chemin).or_else(|| compter_pages_powerpoint(&chemin_str)).unwrap_or(1),
            "ppt" | "pot" | "odp" => compter_pages_powerpoint(&chemin_str).unwrap_or(1),
            _ => 1,
        }
    };

    if nombre > 0 {
        Ok(nombre)
    } else {
        Err(format!("Impossible de determiner le nombre de pages de « {} ».", chemin.file_name().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy()))
    }
}

pub fn nettoyer_processus_office() {
    tuer_processus(&["WINWORD.EXE", "EXCEL.EXE", "POWERPNT.EXE"]);
}

--- FICHIER : D:\Creation\Rust\OmniPrint\src\reseau_utils.rs ---
use std::net::UdpSocket;

pub fn adresse_ip_locale() -> String {
    let mut candidats: Vec<String> = Vec::new();

    for cible in ["8.8.8.8:80", "1.1.1.1:80", "192.168.1.1:80"] {
        if let Ok(s) = UdpSocket::bind("0.0.0.0:0") {
            if s.connect(cible).is_ok() {
                if let Ok(local) = s.local_addr() {
                    let ip = local.ip().to_string();
                    if est_ip_lan(&ip) && !candidats.contains(&ip) {
                        candidats.push(ip);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if let Some(premier) = candidats.first() {
        return premier.clone();
    }

    if let Ok(sortie) = std::process::Command::new("powershell")
        .args([
            "-NoProfile",
            "-NonInteractive",
            "-WindowStyle",
            "Hidden",
            "-Command",
            "Get-NetIPAddress -AddressFamily IPv4 | Where-Object { $_.IPAddress -notlike '169.254.*' -and $_.IPAddress -ne '127.0.0.1' } | Select-Object -ExpandProperty IPAddress",
        ])
        .output()
    {
        if sortie.status.success() {
            let texte = String::from_utf8_lossy(&sortie.stdout);
            for ligne in texte.lines() {
                let ip = ligne.trim().to_string();
                if est_ip_lan(&ip) {
                    return ip;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    "127.0.0.1".into()
}

fn est_ip_lan(ip: &str) -> bool {
    if ip.is_empty() || ip == "0.0.0.0" || ip == "127.0.0.1" {
        return false;
    }
    let parties: Vec<&str> = ip.split('.').collect();
    if parties.len() != 4 {
        return false;
    }
    if parties[0] == "169" && parties[1] == "254" {
        return false;
    }
    true
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::config;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Role {
    Host,
    Client,
}

impl Default for Role {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Role::Host
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ModeCouleur {
    Couleur,
    NoirBlanc,
}

impl Default for ModeCouleur {
    fn default() -> Self {
        ModeCouleur::Couleur
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Orientation {
    Portrait,
    Paysage,
}

impl Default for Orientation {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Orientation::Portrait
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Session {
    pub role: Role,
    pub nom_utilisateur: String,
    pub adresse_host: String,
    pub port: u16,
    pub nom_imprimante: String,
    pub taille_fenetre: Option<(f32, f32)>,
    #[serde(default = "mode_sombre_defaut")]
    pub mode_sombre: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub mode_couleur: ModeCouleur,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub orientation: Orientation,
    #[serde(default = "nom_partage_defaut")]
    pub nom_partage: String,
}

fn mode_sombre_defaut() -> bool {
    true
}

fn nom_partage_defaut() -> String {
    config::NOM_PARTAGE_DEFAUT.to_string()
}

impl Default for Session {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            role: Role::Host,
            nom_utilisateur: std::env::var("USERNAME").unwrap_or_else(|_| "utilisateur".into()),
            adresse_host: "127.0.0.1".into(),
            port: config::DEFAULT_PORT,
            nom_imprimante: String::new(),
            taille_fenetre: None,
            mode_sombre: true,
            mode_couleur: ModeCouleur::Couleur,
            orientation: Orientation::Portrait,
            nom_partage: config::NOM_PARTAGE_DEFAUT.to_string(),
        }
    }
}

impl Session {
    pub fn charger() -> Self {
        let chemin = config::chemin_session();
        match std::fs::read_to_string(&chemin) {
            Ok(s) => {
                let mut session: Self = serde_json::from_str(&s).unwrap_or_default();
                if session.port == 47823 {
                    session.port = config::DEFAULT_PORT;
                    session.sauvegarder();
                }
                session
            }
            Err(_) => Self::default(),
        }
    }

    pub fn sauvegarder(&self) {
        let chemin = config::chemin_session();
        if let Ok(s) = serde_json::to_string_pretty(self) {
            let _ = std::fs::write(chemin, s);
        }
    }
}

    pub const TEXTE: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(232, 235, 245);
    pub const TEXTE_DOUX: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(150, 158, 178);
    pub const ACCENT: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(90, 156, 255);

fn style_commun(ctx: &Context) -> egui::Style {
    let mut style = (*ctx.style()).clone();
    style.spacing.item_spacing = egui::vec2(10.0, 8.0);
    style.spacing.button_padding = egui::vec2(14.0, 8.0);
    style.spacing.interact_size = egui::vec2(40.0, 28.0);
    style.spacing.scroll.bar_width = 8.0;
    style.spacing.scroll.floating = false;
    style.spacing.scroll.bar_inner_margin = 2.0;
    style.spacing.scroll.bar_outer_margin = 0.0;
    style
}

pub fn charger_icone_fenetre() -> Option<egui::IconData> {
    let data = std::fs::read("images/logo.png").ok()?;
    let img = image::load_from_memory(&data).ok()?.to_rgba8();
    let (w, h) = img.dimensions();
    Some(egui::IconData {
        rgba: img.into_raw(),
        width: w,
        height: h,
    })
}

pub fn charger_texture(ctx: &Context, chemin: &str, nom: &str) -> Option<TextureHandle> {
    let data = std::fs::read(chemin).ok()?;
    let img = image::load_from_memory(&data).ok()?.to_rgba8();
    let (w, h) = img.dimensions();
    let pixels = img.into_raw();
    let color_image = egui::ColorImage::from_rgba_unmultiplied([w as usize, h as usize], &pixels);
    Some(ctx.load_texture(nom, color_image, egui::TextureOptions::LINEAR))
}

pub fn separateur(ui: &mut egui::Ui) {
    ui.add_space(10.0);
    let largeur = ui.available_width();
    let (rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(egui::vec2(largeur, 1.0), egui::Sense::hover());
    ui.painter().rect_filled(rect, Rounding::ZERO, Palette::BORDURE);
    ui.add_space(10.0);
}

pub fn badge(ui: &mut egui::Ui, texte: &str, couleur: Color32) {
    let padding = egui::vec2(10.0, 4.0);
    let (rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(
        egui::vec2(texte.len() as f32 * 7.0 + padding.x * 2.0, 22.0),
        egui::Sense::hover(),
    );
    ui.painter()
        .rect_filled(rect, Rounding::same(6.0), couleur.gamma_multiply(0.14));
    ui.painter().rect_stroke(
        rect,
        Rounding::same(6.0),
        Stroke::new(1.0_f32, couleur.gamma_multiply(0.3)),
    );
    ui.painter().text(
        rect.center(),
        egui::Align2::CENTER_CENTER,
        texte,
        egui::FontId::proportional(12.0),
        couleur,
    );
}

pub fn bouton_icone(
    ui: &mut egui::Ui,
    mode_sombre: bool,
    icone: Option<&TextureHandle>,
    texte_secours: &str,
) -> bool {
    let taille = 32.0;
    let (rect, reponse) = ui.allocate_exact_size(egui::vec2(taille, taille), egui::Sense::click());
    let centre = rect.center();
    let rayon = taille / 2.0;
    let fond = if reponse.hovered() {
        if mode_sombre { Color32::from_rgb(42, 48, 64) } else { Color32::from_rgb(232, 238, 250) }
    } else {
        if mode_sombre { Palette::CARTE } else { PaletteClaire::CARTE }
    };
    let bordure = if mode_sombre { Palette::BORDURE } else { PaletteClaire::BORDURE };
    ui.painter().circle_filled(centre, rayon, fond);
    ui.painter().circle_stroke(centre, rayon, Stroke::new(1.0_f32, bordure));

    if let Some(t) = icone {
        let marge = 5.0;
        let rect_img = egui::Rect::from_center_size(
            centre,
            egui::vec2(taille - marge * 2.0, taille - marge * 2.0),
        );
        ui.painter().image(
            t.id(),
            rect_img,
            egui::Rect::from_min_max(egui::pos2(0.0, 0.0), egui::pos2(1.0, 1.0)),
            Color32::WHITE,
        );
    } else {
        let couleur = if mode_sombre { Palette::TEXTE } else { PaletteClaire::TEXTE };
        ui.painter().text(
            centre,
            egui::Align2::CENTER_CENTER,
            texte_secours,
            egui::FontId::proportional(12.0),
            couleur,
        );
    }
    reponse.clicked()
}

pub fn generer_qr(contenu: &str) -> Option<egui::ColorImage> {
    let code = qrcode::QrCode::new(contenu.as_bytes()).ok()?;
    let largeur = code.width();
    let cellules = code.to_colors();
    let marge = 4usize;
    let total = largeur + 2 * marge;
    let mut image = egui::ColorImage::new([total, total], Color32::WHITE);
    for y in 0..largeur {
        for x in 0..largeur {
            let idx = y * largeur + x;
            if cellules[idx] == qrcode::Color::Dark {
                image[(x + marge, y + marge)] = Color32::BLACK;
            }
        }
    }
    Some(image)
}

--- FICHIER : D:\Creation\Rust\OmniPrint\src\web.rs ---
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::mpsc::Sender;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use serde::Deserialize;
use tiny_http::{Header, Method, Response, Server, StatusCode};

use crate::journal::{Entree, Journal, Statut};
use crate::plage_pages::{ModePages, SelectionPages};
use crate::session::{ModeCouleur, Orientation};

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct EtatServeur {
    pub journal: Arc<Mutex<Journal>>,
    pub dossier: PathBuf,
    pub tx: Sender<()>,
    pub imprimante: Arc<Mutex<String>>,
    pub logo: Arc<Vec<u8>>,
}

pub fn demarrer(port: u16, etat: EtatServeur) -> Result<u16, String> {
    let serveur = Server::http(format!("0.0.0.0:{}", port))
        .map_err(|e| format!("Impossible de demarrer sur le port {} : {}", port, e))?;
    let port_reel = serveur
        .server_addr()
        .to_ip()
        .map(|a| a.port())
        .unwrap_or(port);
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        for requete in serveur.incoming_requests() {
            traiter(requete, &etat);
        }
    });
    Ok(port_reel)
}

fn traiter(requete: tiny_http::Request, etat: &EtatServeur) {
    let methode = requete.method().clone();
    let url = requete.url().to_string();

    if methode == Method::Get && (url == "/" || url.starts_with("/?")) {
        let _ = repondre_html(requete, PAGE_CLIENT);
        return;
    }
    if methode == Method::Get && url == "/logo.png" {
        let _ = repondre_png(requete, &etat.logo);
        return;
    }
    if methode == Method::Post && url == "/imprimer" {
        let _ = recevoir_document(requete, etat);
        return;
    }
    if methode == Method::Post && url == "/imprimer-maintenant" {
        let _ = imprimer_maintenant(requete, etat);
        return;
    }
    if methode == Method::Post && url == "/supprimer" {
        let _ = supprimer_document(requete, etat);
        return;
    }
    if methode == Method::Get {
        if let Some(id) = url.strip_prefix("/statut-doc?id=") {
            let _ = repondre_statut_doc(requete, etat, id);
            return;
        }
    }
    if methode == Method::Get {
        if let Some(query) = url.strip_prefix("/apercu?") {
            let _ = repondre_apercu(requete, etat, query);
            return;
        }
    }
    if methode == Method::Get && url == "/favicon.ico" {
        let _ = requete.respond(Response::empty(StatusCode(204)));
        return;
    }
    let _ = requete.respond(Response::from_string("Not found").with_status_code(StatusCode(404)));
}

fn repondre_html(requete: tiny_http::Request, contenu: &str) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    let entete = Header::from_bytes(&b"Content-Type"[..], &b"text/html; charset=utf-8"[..])
        .map_err(|_| std::io::Error::new(std::io::ErrorKind::Other, "header"))?;
    let reponse = Response::from_string(contenu)
        .with_status_code(StatusCode(200))
        .with_header(entete);
    requete.respond(reponse)
}

fn repondre_png(requete: tiny_http::Request, octets: &[u8]) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    let entete = Header::from_bytes(&b"Content-Type"[..], &b"image/png"[..])
        .map_err(|_| std::io::Error::new(std::io::ErrorKind::Other, "header"))?;
    let reponse = Response::from_data(octets.to_vec())
        .with_status_code(StatusCode(200))
        .with_header(entete);
    requete.respond(reponse)
}

fn repondre_json_statut(requete: tiny_http::Request, code: u16, corps: &str) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    let entete = Header::from_bytes(&b"Content-Type"[..], &b"application/json; charset=utf-8"[..])
        .map_err(|_| std::io::Error::new(std::io::ErrorKind::Other, "header"))?;
    let reponse = Response::from_string(corps)
        .with_status_code(StatusCode(code))
        .with_header(entete);
    requete.respond(reponse)
}

fn lire_corps(reader: &mut dyn std::io::Read, tampon: &mut Vec<u8>) -> std::io::Result<usize> {
    std::io::Read::read_to_end(reader, tampon)
}

fn lire_corps_texte(reader: &mut dyn std::io::Read, tampon: &mut String) -> std::io::Result<usize> {
    std::io::Read::read_to_string(reader, tampon)
}

fn recevoir_document(mut requete: tiny_http::Request, etat: &EtatServeur) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    let content_type = requete
        .headers()
        .iter()
        .find(|h| h.field.equiv("Content-Type"))
        .map(|h| h.value.as_str().to_string())
        .unwrap_or_default();

    let boundary = match extraire_boundary(&content_type) {
        Some(b) => b,
        None => {
            return repondre_json_statut(requete, 400, "{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"Requete invalide\"}");
        }
    };

    let mut corps = Vec::new();
    if let Err(e) = lire_corps(&mut requete.as_reader(), &mut corps) {
        return repondre_json_statut(
            requete,
            500,
            &format!("{{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"Lecture impossible : {}\"}}", echapper_json(&e.to_string())),
        );
    }

    if corps.is_empty() {
        return repondre_json_statut(requete, 400, "{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"Corps vide\"}");
    }

    let (nom_fichier, expediteur, contenu) = parser_multipart(&corps, &boundary);

    if contenu.is_empty() || nom_fichier.is_empty() {
        let detail = format!("Fichier vide (nom='{}', taille={})", nom_fichier, contenu.len());
        return repondre_json_statut(requete, 400, &format!("{{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"{}\"}}", echapper_json(&detail)));
    }

    let id = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
    let nom_sur = sanitize(&nom_fichier);
    let chemin = etat.dossier.join(format!("{}_{}", &id[..8], nom_sur));
    
    let ext = chemin.extension().map(|e| e.to_string_lossy().to_lowercase()).unwrap_or_default();
    let est_feuille = matches!(ext.as_str(), "xlsx" | "xlsm" | "xltx" | "xltm" | "xls" | "xlsb" | "ods" | "csv");

    if let Err(e) = std::fs::write(&chemin, &contenu) {
        return repondre_json_statut(requete, 500, &format!("{{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"Ecriture impossible : {}\"}}", echapper_json(&e.to_string())));
    }

    let selection = SelectionPages { est_feuille, ..SelectionPages::default() };
    let entree = Entree {
        id: id.clone(),
        expediteur: if expediteur.trim().is_empty() { "navigateur".into() } else { expediteur },
        nom_fichier: nom_fichier.clone(),
        pages: 0,
        pages_reelles: 0,
        erreur_pages: None,
        version_analyse: 0,
        horodatage: chrono::Local::now(),
        statut: Statut::EnAttente,
        message: chemin.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
        selection,
    };
    {
        let mut j = etat.journal.lock().unwrap();
        j.ajouter(entree);
    }
    let _ = etat.tx.send(());

    let journal_bg = etat.journal.clone();
    let id_bg = id.clone();
    let chemin_bg = chemin.clone();
    let imp_bg = etat.imprimante.lock().unwrap().clone();
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let resultat = crate::printer::compter_pages_document(&chemin_bg, &imp_bg);
        if let Ok(mut j) = journal_bg.lock() {
            if let Some(e) = j.entrees.iter_mut().find(|e| e.id == id_bg) {
                match resultat {
                    Ok(pages) => {
                        e.pages = pages;
                        e.pages_reelles = pages;
                        e.erreur_pages = None;
                    }
                    Err(erreur) => {
                        e.pages = 0;
                        e.pages_reelles = 0;
                        e.erreur_pages = Some(erreur);
                    }
                }
                e.version_analyse = crate::journal::VERSION_ANALYSE_PAGES;
            }
            j.sauvegarder();
        }
    });

    let corps_reponse = format!(
        "{{\"ok\":true,\"id\":\"{}\",\"pages\":0,\"nom\":\"{}\",\"taille\":{},\"est_feuille\":{}}}",
        id, echapper_json(&nom_fichier), contenu.len(), est_feuille
    );
    repondre_json_statut(requete, 200, &corps_reponse)
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct RequeteImpression {
    id: String,
    mode: String,
    orientation: String,
    expediteur: String,
    pages_mode: String,
    pages_cochees: Vec<u32>,
    copies: u32,
}

fn attendre_pages(
    journal: &Arc<Mutex<Journal>>,
    id: &str,
    timeout: Duration,
) -> Result<u32, String> {
    let debut = Instant::now();
    loop {
        {
            let j = journal.lock().unwrap();
            if let Some(e) = j.entrees.iter().find(|e| e.id == id) {
                if e.pages_reelles > 0 {
                    return Ok(e.pages_reelles);
                }
                if let Some(erreur) = e.erreur_pages.as_ref() {
                    return Err(erreur.clone());
                }
            } else {
                return Err("Document introuvable dans la file d'impression.".into());
            }
        }
        if debut.elapsed() > timeout {
            return Err("Le calcul du nombre de pages n'est pas termine. Reessayez dans quelques secondes.".into());
        }
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200));
    }
}

fn imprimer_maintenant(mut requete: tiny_http::Request, etat: &EtatServeur) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    let mut corps = String::new();
    let _ = lire_corps_texte(&mut requete.as_reader(), &mut corps);
    let requete_json: RequeteImpression = match serde_json::from_str(&corps) {
        Ok(r) => r,
        Err(e) => {
            return repondre_json_statut(
                requete,
                400,
                &format!("{{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"JSON invalide : {}\"}}", echapper_json(&e.to_string())),
            );
        }
    };
    if requete_json.id.is_empty() {
        return repondre_json_statut(requete, 400, "{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"ID manquant\"}");
    }
    let imprimante = etat.imprimante.lock().unwrap().clone();
    if imprimante.is_empty() {
        return repondre_json_statut(requete, 400, "{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"Aucune imprimante selectionnee sur le poste hote\"}");
    }

    let total = match attendre_pages(&etat.journal, &requete_json.id, Duration::from_secs(60)) {
        Ok(n) => n,
        Err(erreur) => {
            return repondre_json_statut(
                requete,
                400,
                &format!("{{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"{}\"}}", echapper_json(&erreur)),
            );
        }
    };

    let entree = {
        let j = etat.journal.lock().unwrap();
        j.entrees.iter().find(|e| e.id == requete_json.id).cloned()
    };
    match entree {
        Some(e) if e.statut == Statut::EnAttente => {
            let chemin = PathBuf::from(&e.message);
            if !chemin.exists() {
                let mut j = etat.journal.lock().unwrap();
                j.mettre_a_jour_statut(&e.id, Statut::Erreur, "Fichier absent".into());
                return repondre_json_statut(requete, 400, "{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"Fichier absent sur le poste hote\"}");
            }
            let mode_pages = match requete_json.pages_mode.as_str() {
                "personnalise" => ModePages::Personnalise,
                _ => ModePages::Toutes,
            };
            let selection = SelectionPages {
                mode: mode_pages,
                pages_cochees: requete_json.pages_cochees.clone(),
                copies: if requete_json.copies == 0 { 1 } else { requete_json.copies },
                est_feuille: e.selection.est_feuille,
            };
            let spec = match selection.spec_imprimante(total) {
                Ok(s) => s,
                Err(err) => {
                    return repondre_json_statut(requete, 400, &format!("{{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"{}\"}}", echapper_json(&err)));
                }
            };
            {
                let mut j = etat.journal.lock().unwrap();
                if let Some(e2) = j.entrees.iter_mut().find(|e2| e2.id == e.id) {
                    if !requete_json.expediteur.is_empty() {
                        e2.expediteur = requete_json.expediteur.clone();
                    }
                    e2.selection = selection.clone();
                }
                j.sauvegarder();
            }
            let mode = if requete_json.mode == "noirblanc" { ModeCouleur::NoirBlanc } else { ModeCouleur::Couleur };
            let orientation = if requete_json.orientation == "paysage" { Orientation::Paysage } else { Orientation::Portrait };
            
            crate::printer::imprimer_fichier(
                &imprimante,
                &chemin,
                e.id.clone(),
                etat.journal.clone(),
                mode,
                orientation,
                spec,
                selection.copies_effectives(),
                selection.est_feuille,
            );
            repondre_json_statut(requete, 200, "{\"ok\":true,\"message\":\"Impression lancee\"}")
        }
        Some(_) => repondre_json_statut(requete, 400, "{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"Document deja traite\"}"),
        None => repondre_json_statut(requete, 404, "{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"Document inconnu\"}"),
    }
}

fn supprimer_document(mut requete: tiny_http::Request, etat: &EtatServeur) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    let mut corps = String::new();
    let _ = lire_corps_texte(&mut requete.as_reader(), &mut corps);
    let id = corps.trim().to_string();
    if id.is_empty() {
        return repondre_json_statut(requete, 400, "{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"ID manquant\"}");
    }
    let supprime = {
        let mut j = etat.journal.lock().unwrap();
        j.supprimer(&id)
    };
    if supprime {
        repondre_json_statut(requete, 200, "{\"ok\":true}")
    } else {
        repondre_json_statut(requete, 404, "{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"Document introuvable\"}")
    }
}

fn repondre_statut_doc(requete: tiny_http::Request, etat: &EtatServeur, id: &str) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    let entree = {
        let j = etat.journal.lock().unwrap();
        j.entrees.iter().find(|e| e.id == id).cloned()
    };
    match entree {
        Some(e) => {
            let statut = match e.statut {
                Statut::EnAttente => "EnAttente",
                Statut::EnCours => "EnCours",
                Statut::Imprime => "Imprime",
                Statut::Erreur => "Erreur",
            };
            let total = e.pages_reelles;
            let pret = if e.pages_reelles > 0 { "true" } else { "false" };
            let erreur_pages = e.erreur_pages.as_deref().unwrap_or("");
            let est_feuille = if e.selection.est_feuille { "true" } else { "false" };
            let corps = format!(
                "{{\"ok\":true,\"statut\":\"{}\",\"message\":\"{}\",\"pages\":{},\"pret\":{},\"erreur_pages\":\"{}\",\"est_feuille\":{}}}",
                statut,
                echapper_json(&e.message),
                total,
                pret,
                echapper_json(erreur_pages),
                est_feuille
            );
            repondre_json_statut(requete, 200, &corps)
        }
        None => repondre_json_statut(requete, 404, "{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"Document introuvable\"}"),
    }
}

fn repondre_apercu(requete: tiny_http::Request, etat: &EtatServeur, query: &str) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    let mut id = String::new();
    let mut page = 1u32;
    for partie in query.split('&') {
        let mut it = partie.splitn(2, '=');
        if let (Some(cle), Some(valeur)) = (it.next(), it.next()) {
            match cle {
                "id" => id = valeur.to_string(),
                "page" => page = valeur.parse().unwrap_or(1),
                _ => {}
            }
        }
    }
    if id.is_empty() {
        return repondre_json_statut(requete, 400, "{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"ID manquant\"}");
    }
    let entree = {
        let j = etat.journal.lock().unwrap();
        j.entrees.iter().find(|e| e.id == id).cloned()
    };
    let e = match entree {
        Some(e) => e,
        None => return repondre_json_statut(requete, 404, "{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"Document introuvable\"}"),
    };
    let chemin = PathBuf::from(&e.message);
    match crate::printer::generer_apercu(&chemin, page) {
        Ok(fichier) => {
            let donnees = std::fs::read(&fichier).unwrap_or_default();
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&fichier);
            repondre_png(requete, &donnees)
        }
        Err(erreur) => repondre_json_statut(
            requete,
            500,
            &format!("{{\"ok\":false,\"message\":\"{}\"}}", echapper_json(&erreur)),
        ),
    }
}

fn extraire_boundary(content_type: &str) -> Option<String> {
    for morceau in content_type.split(';') {
        let m = morceau.trim();
        if let Some(rest) = m.strip_prefix("boundary=") {
            return Some(rest.trim().trim_matches('"').to_string());
        }
    }
    None
}

fn parser_multipart(corps: &[u8], boundary: &str) -> (String, String, Vec<u8>) {
    let mut nom_fichier = String::new();
    let mut expediteur = String::new();
    let mut contenu = Vec::new();

    let delimiteur = {
        let mut v = Vec::with_capacity(boundary.len() + 4);
        v.extend_from_slice(b"--");
        v.extend_from_slice(boundary.as_bytes());
        v
    };

    let mut positions: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
    if !delimiteur.is_empty() && corps.len() >= delimiteur.len() {
        let mut i = 0usize;
        while i + delimiteur.len() <= corps.len() {
            if &corps[i..i + delimiteur.len()] == delimiteur.as_slice() {
                positions.push(i);
                i += delimiteur.len();
            } else {
                i += 1;
            }
        }
    }

    if positions.len() < 2 {
        return (nom_fichier, expediteur, contenu);
    }

    for k in 0..positions.len().saturating_sub(1) {
        let debut = positions[k] + delimiteur.len();
        let fin = positions[k + 1];
        if debut >= fin || debut >= corps.len() { continue; }
        let mut curseur = debut;
        if curseur + 1 < fin && corps[curseur] == b'\r' && corps[curseur + 1] == b'\n' { curseur += 2; }
        let entetes_debut = curseur;
        let mut entetes_fin = curseur;
        while entetes_fin + 3 < fin {
            if corps[entetes_fin] == b'\r' && corps[entetes_fin + 1] == b'\n' && corps[entetes_fin + 2] == b'\r' && corps[entetes_fin + 3] == b'\n' { break; }
            entetes_fin += 1;
        }
        if entetes_fin + 3 >= fin { continue; }
        let entetes = String::from_utf8_lossy(&corps[entetes_debut..entetes_fin]).to_string();
        let donnees_debut = entetes_fin + 4;
        let donnees_fin = fin.saturating_sub(2);
        if donnees_debut > donnees_fin { continue; }
        let donnees = corps[donnees_debut..donnees_fin].to_vec();
        let nom_champ = extraire_nom_champ(&entetes);
        if nom_champ == "fichier" {
            nom_fichier = extraire_nom_fichier(&entetes);
            contenu = donnees;
        } else if nom_champ == "expediteur" {
            expediteur = String::from_utf8_lossy(&donnees).trim().to_string();
        }
    }
    (nom_fichier, expediteur, contenu)
}

fn extraire_nom_champ(entetes: &str) -> String {
    for ligne in entetes.lines() {
        if ligne.to_ascii_lowercase().contains("content-disposition") {
            for partie in ligne.split(';') {
                let p = partie.trim();
                if let Some(rest) = p.strip_prefix("name=") {
                    return rest.trim_matches('"').to_string();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    String::new()
}

fn extraire_nom_fichier(entetes: &str) -> String {
    for ligne in entetes.lines() {
        if ligne.to_ascii_lowercase().contains("content-disposition") {
            for partie in ligne.split(';') {
                let p = partie.trim();
                if let Some(rest) = p.strip_prefix("filename=") {
                    return rest.trim_matches('"').to_string();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    "document".into()
}

fn echapper_json(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut sortie = String::with_capacity(s.len());
    for c in s.chars() {
        match c {
            '"' => sortie.push_str("\\\""),
            '\\' => sortie.push_str("\\\\"),
            '\n' => sortie.push_str("\\n"),
            '\r' => sortie.push_str("\\r"),
            '\t' => sortie.push_str("\\t"),
            c if (c as u32) < 0x20 => sortie.push(' '),
            c => sortie.push(c),
        }
    }
    sortie
}

fn sanitize(nom: &str) -> String {
    nom.chars()
        .map(|c| {
            if c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '.' || c == '_' || c == '-' { c } else { '_' }
        })
        .collect()
}

<script>
(function () {
  var zone = document.getElementById('zone');
  var bouton = document.getElementById('bouton-ouvrir');
  var input = document.getElementById('fichier');
  var liste = document.getElementById('liste');
  var nom = document.getElementById('nom');
  var pagesMode = document.getElementById('pages-mode');
  var blocSelection = document.getElementById('bloc-selection');
  var pagesGrid = document.getElementById('pages-grid');
  var titreSelection = document.getElementById('titre-selection');
  var copies = document.getElementById('copies');
  var visuelCourant = null;
  var totalPagesConnues = 0;
  var estFeuilleCourante = false;

  function modeChoisi() {
    var r = document.querySelector('input[name="mode"]:checked');
    return r ? r.value : 'couleur';
  }
  
  function orientationChoisie() {
    var r = document.querySelector('input[name="orientation"]:checked');
    return r ? r.value : 'portrait';
  }

  function formatTaille(o) {
    if (o < 1024) return o + ' o';
    if (o < 1048576) return (o / 1024).toFixed(1) + ' Ko';
    return (o / 1048576).toFixed(1) + ' Mo';
  }

  function construireGrille(total, estFeuille) {
    if (total <= 0) return;
    pagesGrid.innerHTML = '';
    var mot = estFeuille ? 'feuille(s)' : 'page(s)';
    titreSelection.textContent = 'Sélection par case (' + total + ' ' + mot + ' disponible(s))';
    for (var i = 1; i <= total; i++) {
      var lab = document.createElement('label');
      var inp = document.createElement('input');
      inp.type = 'checkbox';
      inp.value = String(i);
      var sp = document.createElement('span');
      sp.textContent = String(i);
      lab.appendChild(inp);
      lab.appendChild(sp);
      pagesGrid.appendChild(lab);
    }
  }

  function majModeAffichage() {
    var m = pagesMode.value;
    blocSelection.style.display = m === 'personnalise' ? 'block' : 'none';
    if (m === 'personnalise') {
      if (totalPagesConnues > 0) {
        construireGrille(totalPagesConnues, estFeuilleCourante);
      } else {
        var mot = estFeuilleCourante ? 'feuille(s)' : 'page(s)';
        titreSelection.textContent = 'Sélection par case (analyse du document en cours...)';
      }
    }
  }

  function lireCoches() {
    var arr = [];
    var inputs = pagesGrid.querySelectorAll('input[type=checkbox]');
    for (var i = 0; i < inputs.length; i++) {
      if (inputs[i].checked) arr.push(parseInt(inputs[i].value, 10));
    }
    return arr;
  }

  function creerItem(fichier) {
    var item = document.createElement('div');
    item.className = 'item';
    var wrap = document.createElement('div');
    wrap.style.flex = '1';
    var nomEl = document.createElement('div');
    nomEl.className = 'nom';
    nomEl.textContent = fichier.name;
    var info = document.createElement('div');
    info.className = 'info';
    info.textContent = formatTaille(fichier.size);
    wrap.appendChild(nomEl);
    wrap.appendChild(info);
    var etat = document.createElement('div');
    etat.className = 'etat';
    etat.textContent = 'Envoi...';
    var btnApercu = document.createElement('button');
    btnApercu.textContent = 'Aperçu';
    btnApercu.disabled = true;
    var btnImp = document.createElement('button');
    btnImp.textContent = 'Imprimer';
    btnImp.disabled = true;
    var btnSup = document.createElement('button');
    btnSup.className = 'danger';
    btnSup.textContent = 'Annuler';
    item.appendChild(wrap);
    item.appendChild(etat);
    item.appendChild(btnApercu);
    item.appendChild(btnImp);
    item.appendChild(btnSup);
    liste.appendChild(item);
    return { racine: item, etat: etat, btnApercu: btnApercu, btnImp: btnImp, btnSup: btnSup, id: null, pages: 0, pret: false };
  }

  function messageErreur(visuel, txt) {
    visuel.etat.className = 'etat etat-err';
    visuel.etat.textContent = txt;
  }

  function supprimer(visuel) {
    if (!visuel.id) { visuel.racine.remove(); return; }
    var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
    xhr.open('POST', '/supprimer', true);
    xhr.setRequestHeader('Content-Type', 'text/plain');
    xhr.onload = function () { visuel.racine.remove(); };
    xhr.onerror = function () { visuel.racine.remove(); };
    xhr.send(visuel.id);
  }

  function suivreStatut(visuel, id) {
    var tentatives = 0;
    function poll() {
      if (tentatives >= 120) return;
      tentatives++;
      var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
      xhr.open('GET', '/statut-doc?id=' + encodeURIComponent(id), true);
      xhr.onload = function () {
        var r = null;
        try { r = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText); } catch (e) {}
        if (r && r.ok) {
          if (r.erreur_pages) {
            messageErreur(visuel, r.erreur_pages);
            visuel.btnImp.disabled = true;
            visuel.btnImp.textContent = 'Indisponible';
            return;
          }
          if (r.pret === true && typeof r.pages === 'number' && r.pages > 0 && (!visuel.pret || visuel.pages !== r.pages)) {
            visuel.pages = r.pages;
            visuel.pret = true;
            if (visuel === visuelCourant) {
              totalPagesConnues = r.pages;
              estFeuilleCourante = r.est_feuille;
              majModeAffichage();
            }
            visuel.etat.className = 'etat etat-ok';
            var mot = r.est_feuille ? 'feuille(s)' : 'page(s)';
            visuel.etat.textContent = 'Prêt (' + r.pages + ' ' + mot + ')';
            visuel.btnImp.disabled = false;
            visuel.btnApercu.disabled = false;
          }
          if (r.statut === 'Imprime') {
            visuel.etat.className = 'etat etat-ok';
            visuel.etat.textContent = 'Imprimé';
            visuel.btnImp.textContent = 'Imprimé';
            visuel.btnImp.disabled = true;
            return;
          }
          if (r.statut === 'Erreur') {
            messageErreur(visuel, r.message || 'Erreur');
            visuel.btnImp.textContent = 'Réessayer';
            visuel.btnImp.disabled = false;
            return;
          }
        }
        setTimeout(poll, 1000);
      };
      xhr.onerror = function () { setTimeout(poll, 1500); };
      xhr.send();
    }
    setTimeout(poll, 400);
  }

  function envoyer(fichier) {
    return new Promise(function (resolve) {
      var visuel = creerItem(fichier);
      visuelCourant = visuel;
      visuel.btnSup.onclick = function () { supprimer(visuel); };
      var donnees = new FormData();
      donnees.append('expediteur', nom.value || 'navigateur');
      donnees.append('fichier', fichier, fichier.name);
      var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
      xhr.open('POST', '/imprimer', true);
      xhr.onload = function () {
        var rep = null;
        try { rep = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText); } catch (e) {}
        if (xhr.status === 200 && rep && rep.ok && rep.id) {
          visuel.id = rep.id;
          visuel.etat.className = 'etat';
          visuel.etat.textContent = 'Analyse du document...';
          suivreStatut(visuel, rep.id);
          visuel.btnApercu.onclick = function () {
            window.open('/apercu?id=' + encodeURIComponent(rep.id) + '&page=1', '_blank');
          };
          visuel.btnImp.onclick = function () {
            visuel.btnImp.disabled = true;
            visuel.btnImp.textContent = 'Impression...';
            visuel.etat.className = 'etat';
            visuel.etat.textContent = 'Impression en cours...';
            var payload = {
              id: rep.id,
              mode: modeChoisi(),
              orientation: orientationChoisie(),
              expediteur: nom.value || 'navigateur',
              pages_mode: pagesMode.value,
              pages_cochees: lireCoches(),
              copies: parseInt(copies.value, 10) || 1
            };
            var xhr2 = new XMLHttpRequest();
            xhr2.open('POST', '/imprimer-maintenant', true);
            xhr2.setRequestHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json');
            xhr2.onload = function () {
              var r2 = null;
              try { r2 = JSON.parse(xhr2.responseText); } catch (e) {}
              if (xhr2.status === 200 && r2 && r2.ok) {
                suivreStatut(visuel, rep.id);
              } else {
                visuel.btnImp.textContent = 'Réessayer';
                visuel.btnImp.disabled = false;
                messageErreur(visuel, (r2 && r2.message) ? r2.message : 'Erreur impression');
              }
            };
            xhr2.onerror = function () {
              visuel.btnImp.textContent = 'Réessayer';
              visuel.btnImp.disabled = false;
              messageErreur(visuel, 'Erreur réseau');
            };
            xhr2.send(JSON.stringify(payload));
          };
        } else {
          messageErreur(visuel, (rep && rep.message) ? rep.message : ('Échec HTTP ' + xhr.status));
        }
        resolve();
      };
      xhr.onerror = function () { messageErreur(visuel, 'Échec réseau'); resolve(); };
      xhr.send(donnees);
    });
  }

  async function traiterFichiers(fichiers) {
    for (var i = 0; i < fichiers.length; i++) {
      var f = fichiers[i];
      if (f.size > 100 * 1024 * 1024) continue;
      await envoyer(f);
    }
  }

  pagesMode.addEventListener('change', majModeAffichage);
  majModeAffichage();

  zone.addEventListener('click', function () { input.click(); });
  bouton.addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); input.click(); });
  input.addEventListener('change', function () {
    if (input.files && input.files.length) traiterFichiers(input.files);
    input.value = '';
  });
  zone.addEventListener('dragover', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); zone.classList.add('survol'); });
  zone.addEventListener('dragleave', function () { zone.classList.remove('survol'); });
  zone.addEventListener('drop', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    zone.classList.remove('survol');
    if (e.dataTransfer && e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files.length) {
      traiterFichiers(e.dataTransfer.files);
    }
  });
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
"#;

--- FICHIER : D:\Creation\Rust\OmniPrint\src\windows_printer.rs ---
use std::process::Command;
use std::os::windows::process::CommandExt;
use std::path::PathBuf;

const CREATE_NO_WINDOW: u32 = 0x08000000;

fn run_ps(script: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
    let output = Command::new("powershell")
        .args(&["-NoProfile", "-ExecutionPolicy", "Bypass", "-Command", script])
        .creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
        .output()
        .map_err(|e| format!("PowerShell launch failed: {}", e))?;

    if output.status.success() {
        Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).trim().to_string())
    } else {
        Err(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).trim().to_string())
    }
}

pub fn ensure_spooler_running() -> Result<(), String> {
    let script = r#"
$svc = Get-Service Spooler -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
if ($svc.Status -ne 'Running') {
    Set-Service Spooler -StartupType Automatic
    Start-Service Spooler
}
if ((Get-Service Spooler).Status -ne 'Running') { throw 'Spooler not running' }
"#;
    run_ps(script).map(|_| ())
}

pub fn ensure_share_enabled(printer_name: &str, share_name: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    let script = format!(r#"
$p = Get-Printer -Name '{}' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
if (-not $p) {{ throw 'Printer not found: {}' }}
if ($p.ShareName -ne '{}') {{
    Set-Printer -Name '{}' -Shared $true -ShareName '{}'
}}
if ((Get-Printer -Name '{}').ShareName -ne '{}') {{ throw 'Share setup failed' }}
"#, printer_name, printer_name, share_name, printer_name, share_name, printer_name, share_name);
    run_ps(&script).map(|_| ())
}

pub fn create_virtual_port(port_name: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    let script = format!(r#"
if (-not (Get-PrinterPort -Name '{}' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {{
    Add-PrinterPort -Name '{}'
}}
"#, port_name, port_name);
    run_ps(&script).map(|_| ())
}

pub fn install_virtual_printer(printer_name: &str, driver_name: &str, port_name: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    let script = format!(r#"
$existing = Get-Printer -Name '{}' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
if ($existing) {{
    if ($existing.PortName -ne '{}' -or $existing.DriverName -ne '{}') {{
        Remove-Printer -Name '{}' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    }} else {{
        exit 0
    }}
}}
Add-Printer -Name '{}' -DriverName '{}' -PortName '{}'
"#, printer_name, port_name, driver_name, printer_name, printer_name, driver_name, port_name);
    run_ps(&script).map(|_| ())
}

pub fn verify_printer_ready(printer_name: &str) -> Result<bool, String> {
    let script = format!(r#"
$p = Get-Printer -Name '{}' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
if (-not $p) {{ 'MISSING' }}
elseif ($p.PrinterStatus -ne 'Normal') {{ 'NOT_READY:' + $p.PrinterStatus }}
else {{ 'READY' }}
"#, printer_name);
    let out = run_ps(&script)?;
    Ok(out == "READY")
}

pub fn setup_omniprint(printer_name: &str, share_name: &str, driver_name: &str, port_name: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    ensure_spooler_running()?;
    create_virtual_port(port_name)?;
    install_virtual_printer(printer_name, driver_name, port_name)?;
    ensure_share_enabled(printer_name, share_name)?;
    if !verify_printer_ready(printer_name)? {
        return Err(format!("Printer '{}' not ready after setup", printer_name));
    }
    Ok(())
}
